# Ultimate Free APIs Collection (2025 Edition)
[![Awesome](https://awesome.re/badge.svg)](https://github.com/sindresorhus/awesome) [![License: CC0-1.0](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-CC0--1.0-brightgreen.svg)](https://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/zero/1.0/) [![Stars](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/BRAIMcodes/awesome-free-apis)](https://github.com/BRAIMcodes/awesome-free-apis)

A **curated, deduplicated, and community-driven** collection of **free public APIs** aggregated from 12+ top sources including [public-apis](https://github.com/public-apis/public-apis), [mixed-analytics](https://mixedanalytics.com/blog/list-actually-free-open-no-auth-needed-apis/), and specialized security/regional lists.

**Why this list?**
- **Comprehensive**: 2000+ APIs from every major category
- **No duplicates**: Intelligent deduplication across all sources
- **English-first**: All content translated/filtered for global accessibility
- **Free-tier focused**: Rate limits and free tier info included

> **Beginner Tip**: Start with "No Auth" APIs for instant testing. **Pro Tip**: Check CORS column for browser compatibility.
